# Individual figure licenses

Project-owned code uses MIT. Research images are excluded from MIT and follow the individual terms below. Preserve attribution, license links and modification notices when reusing any figure.

The gallery is maintained for free, noncommercial academic reference. CC BY-NC-ND figures may not be redistributed as adaptations; verify your intended use against their own license.
